",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"value"}]," 字段；客户端只能看到 ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"present: ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"OPENAI_API_KEY"}],"、",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"}],"、",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"GEMINI_API_KEY"}],"、",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"GOOGLE_API_KEY"}],"、",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY"}],"、",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"OPENROUTER_API_KEY"}],"
